  4 | ,-> export function useEventCreation(
  5 | |       timelineDate: Date,
  6 | |       onAddEvent?: (d: Date, start: number, end: number) => void
  7 | |   ) {
  8 | |       const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  9 | |       const [createPreview, setCreatePreview] = useState<{ start: number; end: number } | null>(null);
 10 | |   
 11 | |       const onGridPointerDown = useCallback((e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
 12 | |           if (!(e.target instanceof Element)) return;
 13 | |           if (e.target.closest(".day-event") || e.target.closest(".day-now")) return;
 14 | |           if (e.button !== 0) return;
 15 | |           e.preventDefault();
 16 | |   
 17 | |           const grid = containerRef.current;
 18 | |           if (!grid) return;
 19 | |           const rect = grid.getBoundingClientRect();

 21 | |           const startMin = Math.round(startY / PX_PER_MIN / SNAP_MIN) * SNAP_MIN;
 22 | |   
 23 | |           let active = false;
 24 | |   
 25 | |           const move = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
 26 | |               active = true;
 27 | |               const currentY = ev.clientY - rect.top;
 28 | |               const moveMin = Math.round(currentY / PX_PER_MIN / SNAP_MIN) * SNAP_MIN;
 29 | |               const s = Math.min(startMin, moveMin);
 30 | |               const eMin = Math.max(startMin, moveMin);
 31 | |               setCreatePreview({
 32 | |                   start: s,
 33 | |                   end: eMin === s ? s + SNAP_MIN : eMin,
 34 | |               });
 35 | |           };
 36 | |   
 37 | |           const up = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
 38 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
 39 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
 40 | |               if (!active) {
 41 | |                   if (onAddEvent) {
 42 | |                       onAddEvent(timelineDate, startMin, startMin + 60);
 43 | |                   }
 44 | |                   return;
 45 | |               }
 46 | |               const currentY = ev.clientY - rect.top;
 47 | |               const moveMin = Math.round(currentY / PX_PER_MIN / SNAP_MIN) * SNAP_MIN;
 48 | |               const s = Math.min(startMin, moveMin);
 49 | |               const eMin = Math.max(startMin, moveMin);
 50 | |               const finalEnd = eMin === s ? s + SNAP_MIN : eMin;
 51 | |               setCreatePreview(null);
 52 | |               if (onAddEvent) {
 53 | |                   onAddEvent(timelineDate, s, finalEnd);
 54 | |               }
 55 | |           };
 56 | |   
 57 | |           window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
 58 | |           window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
 59 | |       }, [timelineDate, onAddEvent]);

 31 |     
 32 | ,->     private async processPushQueue() {
 33 | |           if (this.pushQueue.length === 0) return;
 34 | |           syncState.isPushing = true;
 35 | |   
 36 | |           const settings = this.getSettings();
 37 | |   
 38 | |           while (this.pushQueue.length > 0) {
 39 | |               const taskOrEvent = this.pushQueue.peek();
 40 | |               if (!taskOrEvent) {
 41 | |                   this.pushQueue.shift();
 42 | |                   continue;
 43 | |               }
 44 | |               try {
 45 | |                   if (taskOrEvent.type === SyncType.Task && settings.enableTasksSync === false) {
 46 | |                       this.pushQueue.shift();
 47 | |                       continue;
 48 | |                   }
 49 | |                   if (taskOrEvent.type === SyncType.Event && settings.enableCalendarSync === false) {
 50 | |                       this.pushQueue.shift();
 51 | |                       continue;
 52 | |                   }
 53 | |   
 54 | |                   if (taskOrEvent.type === SyncType.Task) {
 55 | |                       await this.taskSync.processPushItem(taskOrEvent);
 56 | |                   } else if (taskOrEvent.type === SyncType.Event) {
 57 | |                       await this.eventSync.processPushItem(taskOrEvent);
 58 | |                   }
 59 | |                   this.pushQueue.shift();
 60 | |               } catch (e: unknown) {
 61 | |                   console.error("Push failed, keeping in queue", e);
 62 | |                   syncState.error = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Error syncing item to Google.";
 63 | |                   break;
 64 | |               }
 65 | |           }
 66 | |   
 67 | |           syncState.isPushing = false;

 15 |     
 16 | ,-> export function useStopwatchKeyboard(
 17 | |       selectorOpen: boolean,
 18 | |       setSelectorOpen: (val: boolean | ((prev: boolean) => boolean)) => void,
 19 | |       actionsRef: React.MutableRefObject<StopwatchActions | null>,
 20 | |       activeTask: AppTask | null | undefined,
 21 | |       allowNoTask: boolean
 22 | |   ) {
 23 | |       useEffect(() => {
 24 | |           const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
 25 | |               if (isInputTarget(e.target)) return;
 26 | |   
 27 | |               const isModifier = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
 28 | |   
 29 | |               switch (e.code) {
 30 | |                   case "Space":
 31 | |                       if (!selectorOpen) {
 32 | |                           e.preventDefault();
 33 | |                           actionsRef.current?.toggle();
 34 | |                       }
 35 | |                       break;
 36 | |                   case "KeyR":
 37 | |                       if (isModifier) {
 38 | |                           e.preventDefault();
 39 | |                           actionsRef.current?.reset();
 40 | |                       }
 41 | |                       break;
 42 | |                   case "Enter":
 43 | |                       if (isModifier) {
 44 | |                           e.preventDefault();
 45 | |                           setSelectorOpen((prev: boolean) => !prev);
 46 | |                       }
 47 | |                       break;
 48 | |                   case "Escape":
 49 | |                       if (selectorOpen && (activeTask || allowNoTask)) {
 50 | |                           e.preventDefault();
 51 | |                           setSelectorOpen(false);
 52 | |                       }
 53 | |                       break;
 54 | |               }
 55 | |           };
 56 | |   
 57 | |           const pressed = new Set<string>();
 58 | |           const handleDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
 59 | |               if (isInputTarget(e.target)) return;
 60 | |               pressed.add(e.code);
 61 | |               if (pressed.has("ShiftLeft") && pressed.has("ShiftRight")) {
 62 | |                   e.preventDefault();
 63 | |                   actionsRef.current?.startBreak();
 64 | |               }
 65 | |           };
 66 | |           const handleUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
 67 | |               pressed.delete(e.code);
 68 | |           };
 69 | |   
 70 | |           window.addEventListener("keydown", handleDown);
 71 | |           window.addEventListener("keyup", handleUp);
 72 | |           window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
 73 | |           return () => {
 74 | |               window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleDown);
 75 | |               window.removeEventListener("keyup", handleUp);
 76 | |               window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
 77 | |           };
 78 | |       }, [selectorOpen, activeTask, allowNoTask, actionsRef, setSelectorOpen]);
 79 | `-> }

  19 | ,-> export function EventBlock<T extends import("./types").DragState = import("./types").DragState>({
  20 | |       event,
  21 | |       task,
  22 | |       lane,
  23 | |       lanes,
  24 | |       onResize,
  25 | |       onMove,
  26 | |       onEventClick,
  27 | |   }: EventBlockProps<T>) {
  28 | |       const [dragOffset, setDragOffset] = useState<number | null>(null);
  29 | |   
  30 | |       const title = event.title;
  31 | |       const pri = event.priority;
  32 | |   
  33 | |       const onResizeStart = (edge: "top" | "bottom") => (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
  34 | |           e.stopPropagation();
  35 | |           e.preventDefault();
  36 | |           const startY = e.clientY;
  37 | |           const startStart = event.start;
  38 | |           const startEnd = event.end || 0;
  39 | |           const move = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
  40 | |               const dy = ev.clientY - startY;
  41 | |               const dm = Math.round(dy / PX_PER_MIN / SNAP_MIN) * SNAP_MIN;
  42 | |               if (edge === "bottom") {
  43 | |                   const newEnd = Math.max(
  44 | |                       startStart + SNAP_MIN,
  45 | |                       Math.min(24 * 60, startEnd + dm),
  46 | |                   );
  47 | |                   onResize(event.id, startStart, newEnd);
  48 | |               } else {
  49 | |                   const newStart = Math.max(
  50 | |                       0,
  51 | |                       Math.min(startEnd - SNAP_MIN, startStart + dm),
  52 | |                   );
  53 | |                   onResize(event.id, newStart, startEnd);
  54 | |               }
  55 | |           };
  56 | |           const up = () => {
  57 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
  58 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
  59 | |           };
  60 | |           window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
  61 | |           window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
  62 | |       };
  63 | |   
  64 | |       const onBodyDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
  65 | |           if (!(e.target instanceof Element)) return;
  66 | |           if (e.target.closest(".day-event-handle")) return;
  67 | |           if (e.button !== 0) return;
  68 | |           e.preventDefault();
  69 | |           const startY = e.clientY;
  70 | |           const startStart = event.start || 0;
  71 | |           const startEnd = event.end || 0;
  72 | |           let moved = false;
  73 | |           let finalDm = 0;
  74 | |           const move = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
  75 | |               const dy = ev.clientY - startY;
  76 | |               if (!moved && Math.abs(dy) < 4) return;
  77 | |               moved = true;
  78 | |               const dm = Math.round(dy / PX_PER_MIN / SNAP_MIN) * SNAP_MIN;
  79 | |               const minDm = -startStart;
  80 | |               const maxDm = 24 * 60 - startEnd;
  81 | |               finalDm = Math.max(minDm, Math.min(maxDm, dm));
  82 | |               setDragOffset(finalDm);
  83 | |           };
  84 | |           const up = () => {
  85 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
  86 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
  87 | |               if (!moved) {
  88 | |                   if (onEventClick) onEventClick(event);
  89 | |                   return;
  90 | |               }
  91 | |               setDragOffset(null);
  92 | |               if (finalDm !== 0) {
  93 | |                   onMove(
  94 | |                       event.id,
  95 | |                       startStart + finalDm,

     ,-[src/screens/minitracker/MiniTrackerScreen.tsx:46:1]
  45 |     
  46 | ,-> function GuzeyClock({ now, running, taskName }: { now: number; running: boolean; taskName: string }) {
  47 | |       const d = new Date(now);
  48 | |       const h = d.getHours();
  49 | |       const min = d.getMinutes();
  50 | |       const isLongBreak = h % 3 === 0;
  51 | |       let breakState = "WORK";
  52 | |       let nextMin = 60;
  53 | |   
  54 | |       if (isLongBreak && min < 35) {
  55 | |           breakState = "BREAK";
  56 | |           nextMin = 35;
  57 | |       } else if (min >= 0 && min < 5) {
  58 | |           breakState = "BREAK";
  59 | |           nextMin = 5;
  60 | |       } else if (min >= 5 && min < 30) {
  61 | |           breakState = "WORK";
  62 | |           nextMin = 30;
  63 | |       } else if (min >= 30 && min < 35) {
  64 | |           breakState = "BREAK";
  65 | |           nextMin = 35;
  66 | |       } else {
  67 | |           breakState = "WORK";
  68 | |           nextMin = 60;
  69 | |       }
  70 | |   
  71 | |       let target = new Date(d);
  72 | |       target.setSeconds(0);
  73 | |       target.setMilliseconds(0);
  74 | |       if (nextMin === 60) {
  75 | |           target.setMinutes(0);
  76 | |           target.setHours(target.getHours() + 1);
  77 | |       } else {
  78 | |           target.setMinutes(nextMin);
  79 | |       }
  80 | |   
  81 | |       const remainingMs = target.getTime() - d.getTime();
  82 | |       const remS = Math.floor(remainingMs / 1000);
  83 | |       const remM = Math.floor(remS / 60);
  84 | |       const finalS = remS % 60;
  85 | |   
  86 | |       if (!running) {
  87 | |           return (
  88 | |               <div style={{ color: "var(--fg-dim)" }}>
  89 | |                   TRACKING PAUSED
  90 | |               </div>
  91 | |           );
  92 | |       } else if (breakState === "BREAK") {
  93 | |           return (
  94 | |               <div style={{ color: "var(--tag-green)" }}>
  95 | |                   {PAD2(remM)}:{PAD2(finalS)} left for break
  96 | |               </div>
  97 | |           );
  98 | |       } else {
  99 | |           return (
 100 | |               <div>
 101 | |                   {PAD2(remM)}:{PAD2(finalS)} left working for {taskName}
 102 | |               </div>
 103 | |           );
 104 | |       }
 105 | `-> }

   8 | ,-> export function useDragAndDrop(

  10 | |       setInspectorState: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<{ type: string, id: string } | null>>,
  11 | |       createEvent: (task: AppTask, date: import("../../core/domain/models").DateString, start: number, duration: number, isAllDay?: boolean) => void
  12 | |   ) {
  13 | |       const [, setEvents] = useEvents();
  14 | |       const [calendars] = useCalendars();
  15 | |       
  16 | |       const [dragState, setDragState] = useState<PlanDragState | null>(null);
  17 | |       const dragRef = useRef<PlanDragState | null>(null);
  18 | |       dragRef.current = dragState;
  19 | |   
  20 | |       const onTaskDragStart = (e: React.PointerEvent<Element> | React.MouseEvent<Element, MouseEvent>, task: AppTask) => {
  21 | |           e.preventDefault();
  22 | |           const start = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
  23 | |           let active = false;
  24 | |   
  25 | |           const move = (ev: PointerEvent | MouseEvent) => {
  26 | |               if (!active) {
  27 | |                   const dx = ev.clientX - start.x;
  28 | |                   const dy = ev.clientY - start.y;
  29 | |                   if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 5) return;
  30 | |                   active = true;
  31 | |               }
  32 | |               const el = document.elementFromPoint(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
  33 | |               const target = resolveDropTarget(
  34 | |                   el,
  35 | |                   ev.clientX,
  36 | |                   ev.clientY,
  37 | |                   task,
  38 | |                   undefined,
  39 | |               );
  40 | |               setDragState({
  41 | |                   task,
  42 | |                   pointerX: ev.clientX,
  43 | |                   pointerY: ev.clientY,
  44 | |                   target,
  45 | |               });
  46 | |           };
  47 | |   
  48 | |           const up = () => {
  49 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
  50 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
  51 | |               if (!active) {
  52 | |                   setInspectorState({ type: "task", id: task.id });
  53 | |               } else {
  54 | |                   const ds = dragRef.current;
  55 | |                   if (ds && ds.target) {
  56 | |                       if (ds.target.kind === "grid-day" && ds.target.date) {
  57 | |                           createEvent(
  58 | |                               task,
  59 | |                               ds.target.date,
  60 | |                               9 * 60,
  61 | |                               task.est || 60,
  62 | |                               true,
  63 | |                           );
  64 | |                       } else if (ds.target.kind === "day-time" && ds.target.minute !== undefined) {

  66 | |                               task,
  67 | |                               ymd(timelineDate),
  68 | |                               ds.target.minute,
  69 | |                               task.est || 60,
  70 | |                               false,
  71 | |                           );
  72 | |                       }
  73 | |                   }
  74 | |               }
  75 | |               setDragState(null);
  76 | |           };
  77 | |           window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
  78 | |           window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
  79 | |       };
  80 | |   
  81 | |       const onEventDragStart = (e: React.PointerEvent<Element> | React.MouseEvent<Element, MouseEvent>, eventToMove: AppEvent, task?: AppTask | null) => {
  82 | |           const calId = eventToMove.googleCalendarId || "primary";
  83 | |           const cal = calendars.find((c) => c.id === calId);
  84 | |           if (cal && (cal.accessRole === "reader" || cal.accessRole === "freeBusyReader")) {
  85 | |               setInspectorState({ type: "event", id: eventToMove.id });
  86 | |               return;
  87 | |           }
  88 | |   
  89 | |           e.preventDefault();
  90 | |           e.stopPropagation();
  91 | |           const start = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };

  95 | |               if (!active) {
  96 | |                   const dx = ev.clientX - start.x;
  97 | |                   const dy = ev.clientY - start.y;

  99 | |                   active = true;

 101 | |               const el = document.elementFromPoint(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
 102 | |               const target = resolveDropTarget(

 106 | |                   task,

 108 | |               );

 122 | |                   setInspectorState({ type: "event", id: eventToMove.id });
 123 | |               } else {
 124 | |                   const ds = dragRef.current;
 125 | |                   if (ds && ds.target) {
 126 | |                       if (ds.target.kind === "grid-day" && ds.target.date) {
 127 | |                           setEvents((prev: AppEvent[]) =>
 128 | |                               prev.map((evnt: AppEvent) =>
 129 | |                                   evnt.id === eventToMove.id

 156 | |               }
 157 | |               setDragState(null);
 158 | |           };

     ,-[src/screens/do/rest/RestScreen.tsx:13:8]
  12 |     
  13 | ,-> export function RestScreen() {
  14 | |   
  15 | |   
  16 | |       // Reset every visit: don't persist checks across reloads, but keep custom items.
  17 | |       const [items, setItems] = useRestItems();
  18 | |       // Local check state — not persisted, resets every page load
  19 | |       const [checked, setChecked] = React.useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  20 | |       const [editing, setEditing] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  21 | |       const [adding, setAdding] = React.useState(false);
  22 | |       const [draft, setDraft] = React.useState("");
  23 | |   
  24 | |       const toggle = (id: string) => setChecked((c) => ({ ...c, [id]: !c[id] }));
  25 | |       const updateItem = (id: string, title: string) => {
  26 | |           if (!title.trim()) {
  27 | |               setItems((its) => its.filter((i) => i.id !== id));
  28 | |           } else {
  29 | |               setItems((its) =>
  30 | |                   its.map((i) =>
  31 | |                       i.id === id ? { ...i, title: title.trim() } : i,
  32 | |                   ),
  33 | |               );
  34 | |           }
  35 | |           setEditing(null);
  36 | |       };
  37 | |       const addItem = () => {
  38 | |           const t = draft.trim();
  39 | |           if (!t) {
  40 | |               setAdding(false);
  41 | |               setDraft("");
  42 | |               return;
  43 | |           }
  44 | |           setItems((its) => [
  45 | |               ...its,
  46 | |               { id: `r${Date.now()}`, title: t, type: "check" },
  47 | |           ]);
  48 | |           setDraft("");
  49 | |           setAdding(false);
  50 | |       };
  51 | |       const removeItem = (id: string) =>
  52 | |           setItems((its) => its.filter((i) => i.id !== id));
  53 | |   
  54 | |       const allChecked = items.length > 0 && items.every((i) => checked[i.id]);
  55 | |   
  56 | |       return (
  57 | |           <div
  58 | |               className="rest-main"
  59 | |               style={{ padding: "24px", minHeight: "auto" }}
  60 | |           >
  61 | |               <div className="rest-stage" style={{ margin: "0 auto" }}>
  62 | |                   <header className="rest-header">
  63 | |                       <div className="rest-bracket-row">
  64 | |                           <span className="bracket">┌─</span>
  65 | |                           <span className="rest-label">
  66 | |                               REST · {DOW_SHORT[TODAY.getDay()].toLowerCase()}{" "}
  67 | |                               {MONTHS[TODAY.getMonth()].toLowerCase()}{" "}
  68 | |                               {TODAY.getDate()}
  69 | |                           </span>
  70 | |                           <span className="bracket">─┐</span>
  71 | |                       </div>
  72 | |                       <h1 className="rest-title">
  73 | |                           Give yourself a pat on the back.
  74 | |                       </h1>
  75 | |                       <p className="rest-sub">
  76 | |                           Even a small <em>“attaboy”</em> or <em>“attagirl”</em>{" "}
  77 | |                           makes a difference.
  78 | |                       </p>
  79 | |                   </header>
  80 | |   
  81 | |                   <ol className="rest-list">
  82 | |                       {items.map((item) => (
  83 | |                           <li
  84 | |                               key={item.id}
  85 | |                               className={`rest-item ${checked[item.id] ? "is-checked" : ""}`}
  86 | |                           >
  87 | |                               <button
  88 | |                                   className="rest-checkbox"
  89 | |                                   onClick={() => toggle(item.id)}
  90 | |                                   aria-pressed={!!checked[item.id]}
  91 | |                               >
  92 | |                                   <span className="rest-checkbox-box">
  93 | |                                       {checked[item.id] ? (
  94 | |                                           <span className="rest-check">✓</span>
  95 | |                                       ) : (
  96 | |                                           <span className="rest-check-empty">
  97 | |                                               {" "}
  98 | |                                           </span>
  99 | |                                       )}
 100 | |                                   </span>
 101 | |                               </button>
 102 | |                               {editing === item.id ? (
 103 | |                                   <input
 104 | |                                       ref={(r) => { if (r && editing === item.id) r.focus(); }}
 105 | |                                       className="rest-item-input"
 106 | |                                       defaultValue={item.title}
 107 | |                                       onBlur={(e) =>
 108 | |                                           updateItem(item.id, e.target.value)
 109 | |                                       }
 110 | |                                       onKeyDown={(e) => {
 111 | |                                           if (e.key === "Enter") {
 112 | |                                               if (e.currentTarget instanceof HTMLElement) e.currentTarget.blur();
 113 | |                                           }
 114 | |                                           if (e.key === "Escape")
 115 | |                                               setEditing(null);
 116 | |                                       }}
 117 | |                                   />
 118 | |                               ) : (
 119 | |                                   <button
 120 | |                                       type="button"
 121 | |                                       className="rest-item-title"
 122 | |                                       onClick={() => setEditing(item.id)}
 123 | |                                       style={{ background: "none", border: "none", font: "inherit", color: "inherit", padding: 0, textAlign: "left", cursor: "text" }}
 124 | |                                   >
 125 | |                                       {item.title}
 126 | |                                   </button>
 127 | |                               )}
 128 | |                               <button
 129 | |                                   className="rest-item-x"
 130 | |                                   onClick={() => removeItem(item.id)}
 131 | |                                   title="Remove"
 132 | |                                   tabIndex={-1}
 133 | |                               >
 134 | |                                   ×
 135 | |                               </button>
 136 | |                           </li>
 137 | |                       ))}
 138 | |                       <li className="rest-item rest-item-add">
 139 | |                           <span className="rest-checkbox rest-checkbox-add">
 140 | |                               <span className="rest-checkbox-box rest-checkbox-box-add">
 141 | |                                   +
 142 | |                               </span>
 143 | |                           </span>
 144 | |                           {adding ? (
 145 | |                               <input
 146 | |                                   ref={(r) => { if (r && adding) r.focus(); }}
 147 | |                                   className="rest-item-input"
 148 | |                                   placeholder="new rest item…"
 149 | |                                   value={draft}
 150 | |                                   onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
 151 | |                                   onBlur={addItem}
 152 | |                                   onKeyDown={(e) => {
 153 | |                                       if (e.key === "Enter") addItem();
 154 | |                                       if (e.key === "Escape") {
 155 | |                                           setAdding(false);

 167 | |                           )}
 168 | |                       </li>

 192 | |                       </button>
 193 | |                   </section>
 194 | |   
 195 | |                   {allChecked && (

   4 | ,-> export function expandEventsForView(
   5 | |       baseEvents: AppEvent[],
   6 | |       viewStartDate: Date,
   7 | |       viewEndDate: Date,
   8 | |   ) {
   9 | |       const flattenedInstances: AppEvent[] = [];
  10 | |   
  11 | |       baseEvents.forEach((event) => {
  12 | |           if (!event.rrule) {
  13 | |               flattenedInstances.push(event);
  14 | |               return;
  15 | |           }
  16 | |   
  17 | |           try {
  18 | |               const rule = RRule.fromString(event.rrule);
  19 | |               // RRule works with UTC inherently unless adjusted, we should pass Date objects
  20 | |               // representing the local time, but rrule.js usually prefers UTC dates for local calculations.
  21 | |               // For simplicity, we just use the viewStartDate and viewEndDate directly.
  22 | |               const generatedDates = rule.between(
  23 | |                   viewStartDate,
  24 | |                   viewEndDate,
  25 | |                   true,
  26 | |               );
  27 | |   
  28 | |               generatedDates.forEach((date) => {
  29 | |                   // Simple string format YYYY-MM-DD
  30 | |                   const y = date.getFullYear();
  31 | |                   const m = (date.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, "0");
  32 | |                   const d = date.getDate().toString().padStart(2, "0");
  33 | |                   const dateStr = `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
  34 | |   
  35 | |                   const exceptionOverride = baseEvents.find(
  36 | |                       (e) =>
  37 | |                           e.recurringEventId === event.id &&
  38 | |                           e.originalStartDate === dateStr,
  39 | |                   );
  40 | |   
  41 | |                   if (exceptionOverride) {
  42 | |                       if (!exceptionOverride.cancelled) {
  43 | |                           flattenedInstances.push(exceptionOverride);
  44 | |                       }
  45 | |                   } else {
  46 | |                       // If the event spans multiple days (endDate !== date), we should preserve duration.
  47 | |                       // In this simple implementation, we just shift date and endDate.
  48 | |                       let newEndDate = dateStr;
  49 | |                       if (event.endDate && event.endDate !== event.date) {
  50 | |                           const startDt = new Date(event.date);
  51 | |                           const endDt = new Date(event.endDate);
  52 | |                           const diffDays = Math.round(
  53 | |                               (endDt.getTime() - startDt.getTime()) /
  54 | |                                   (1000 * 3600 * 24),
  55 | |                           );
  56 | |   
  57 | |                           const newEndDt = new Date(date.getTime());
  58 | |                           newEndDt.setDate(newEndDt.getDate() + diffDays);
  59 | |                           const ey = newEndDt.getFullYear();
  60 | |                           const em = (newEndDt.getMonth() + 1)
  61 | |                               .toString()
  62 | |                               .padStart(2, "0");
  63 | |                           const ed = newEndDt
  64 | |                               .getDate()
  65 | |                               .toString()
  66 | |                               .padStart(2, "0");
  67 | |                           newEndDate = `${ey}-${em}-${ed}`;
  68 | |                       }
  69 | |   
  70 | |                       if (event.type === "log") {
  71 | |                           flattenedInstances.push({
  72 | |                               ...event,
  73 | |                               id: `${event.id}_${date.getTime()}`,
  74 | |                               date: dateStr,
  75 | |                               isInstance: true,
  76 | |                               baseEventId: event.id,
  77 | |                           });
  78 | |                       } else {
  79 | |                           flattenedInstances.push({
  80 | |                               ...event,
  81 | |                               id: `${event.id}_${date.getTime()}`,
  82 | |                               date: dateStr,
  83 | |                               endDate: newEndDate,
  84 | |                               isInstance: true,
  85 | |                               baseEventId: event.id,
  86 | |                           });
  87 | |                       }
  88 | |                   }
  89 | |               });
  90 | |           } catch (e) {
  91 | |               console.error(
  92 | |                   "Failed to parse RRULE for event",
  93 | |                   event.title || event.id,
  94 | |                   e,
  95 | |               );
  96 | |               // Fallback: just show the base event if rule is invalid
  97 | |               flattenedInstances.push(event);
  98 | |           }
  99 | |       });
 100 | |   
 101 | |       return flattenedInstances;

 143 | |               const m = (endDt.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, "0");
 144 | |               const d = endDt.getDate().toString().padStart(2, "0");
 145 | |               endYMD = `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
 146 | |           }
 147 | |   
 148 | |           const startStr = `${dateStr}T${startH.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:${startM.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
 149 | |           const endStr = `${endYMD}T${endH.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:${endM.toString().padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
 150 | |           const timeZone = Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;
 151 | |   
 152 | |           const r: gapi.client.calendar.Event = {
 153 | |               summary: title || "Taskroot Event",
 154 | |               start: { dateTime: startStr, timeZone },
 155 | |               end: { dateTime: endStr, timeZone },
 156 | |               description: localEvent.description || "",
 157 | |               extendedProperties: {
 158 | |                   private: {
 159 | |                       taskrootEventId: localEvent.id,
 160 | |                       ...(localEvent.taskId ? { taskId: localEvent.taskId } : {}),
 161 | |                       type: localEvent.type || "",
 162 | |                   },
 163 | |               },
 164 | |           };
 165 | |   
 166 | |           if (localEvent.rrule) {
 167 | |               r.recurrence = [`RRULE:${localEvent.rrule}`];
 168 | |           }
 169 | |           return r;

   3 |     
   4 | ,-> describe("computeFilterDefaults", () => {
   5 | |       it("returns empty defaults for no filters", () => {
   6 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults([])).toEqual({});
   7 | |       });
   8 | |   
   9 | |       it('infers status from "is" filter', () => {
  10 | |           const filters = [{ column: "status", operator: "is", value: "done" }];
  11 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ status: "done" });
  12 | |       });
  13 | |   
  14 | |       it('infers priority from "is" filter', () => {
  15 | |           const filters = [{ column: "priority", operator: "is", value: 3 }];
  16 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ priority: 3 });
  17 | |       });
  18 | |   
  19 | |       it('picks fallback status when "is not" filter excludes todo', () => {
  20 | |           const filters = [
  21 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: "todo" },
  22 | |           ];
  23 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ status: "next-up" }); // Because 'next-up' is next in fallback list
  24 | |       });
  25 | |   
  26 | |       it('picks fallback status when multiple "is not" filters exclude top choices', () => {
  27 | |           const filters = [
  28 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: "todo" },
  29 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: "next-up" },
  30 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: "doing" },
  31 | |           ];
  32 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ status: "done" });
  33 | |       });
  34 | |   
  35 | |       it('picks fallback priority when "is not" excludes 1', () => {
  36 | |           const filters = [{ column: "priority", operator: "is not", value: 1 }];
  37 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ priority: 2 });
  38 | |       });
  39 | |   
  40 | |       it('does not apply conflicting "is" requirements', () => {
  41 | |           const filters = [
  42 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is", value: "todo" },
  43 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is", value: "done" },
  44 | |           ];
  45 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({});
  46 | |       });
  47 | |   
  48 | |       it("does not apply value if it is both required and excluded", () => {
  49 | |           const filters = [
  50 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is", value: "done" },
  51 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: "done" },
  52 | |           ];
  53 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({});
  54 | |       });
  55 | |   
  56 | |       it("adds tags correctly and ignores excluded tags", () => {
  57 | |           const filters = [
  58 | |               { column: "tag", operator: "is", value: "bug" },
  59 | |               { column: "tag", operator: "is", value: "feature" },
  60 | |               { column: "tag", operator: "is not", value: "bug" },
  61 | |               { column: "tag", operator: "is not", value: "urgent" },
  62 | |           ];
  63 | |           // "bug" is excluded, so only "feature" should be included
  64 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ tags: ["feature"] });
  65 | |       });
  66 | |   
  67 | |       it("handles array values for 'is' status filtering correctly", () => {
  68 | |           const filters = [
  69 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is", value: ["todo", "next-up"] },
  70 | |           ];
  71 | |           // When multiple statuses are requested, computeFilterDefaults doesn't apply a single status
  72 | |           // since a single task can't have multiple statuses simultaneously.
  73 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({});
  74 | |       });
  75 | |   
  76 | |       it("handles array values for 'is not' status filtering", () => {
  77 | |           const filters = [
  78 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: ["todo", "next-up", "doing"] },
  79 | |           ];
  80 | |           // Excludes everything before "done"
  81 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ status: "done" });
  82 | |       });
  83 | |   
  84 | |       it("handles array values for tag filtering", () => {
  85 | |           const filters = [
  86 | |               { column: "tag", operator: "is", value: ["bug", "urgent"] },
  87 | |           ];
  88 | |           // "bug" and "urgent" are both added to defaults
  89 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ tags: ["bug", "urgent"] });
  90 | |       });
  91 | |   
  92 | |       it("handles array values for tag exclusion", () => {
  93 | |           const filters = [
  94 | |               { column: "tag", operator: "is", value: ["bug", "feature", "ui"] },
  95 | |               { column: "tag", operator: "is not", value: ["bug", "ui"] },
  96 | |           ];
  97 | |           // "bug" and "ui" are excluded, leaving only "feature"
  98 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ tags: ["feature"] });
  99 | |       });
 100 | |   
 101 | |       it("handles mixed single and array values for exclusion", () => {
 102 | |           const filters = [
 103 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: ["todo"] },
 104 | |               { column: "status", operator: "is not", value: "next-up" },
 105 | |           ];
 106 | |           // "todo" and "next-up" are excluded, so fallback is "doing"
 107 | |           expect(computeFilterDefaults(filters)).toEqual({ status: "doing" });

  35 | |       calendars: { id: string; accessRole?: string }[]

  47 | |       const currentItem = currentTask || currentEvent || null;

  53 | |           const cal = calendars.find((c) => c.id === calId);

  62 | |   
  63 | |       const deleteTask = React.useCallback((id: string) => setTasks((ts) => ts.filter((t) => t.id !== id)), [setTasks]);
  64 | |   
  65 | |       const updateEvent = React.useCallback((id: string, updates: Partial<AppEvent>) =>

  67 | |

  69 | |           setEvents((es) => es.filter((e) => e.id !== id)), [setEvents]);
  70 | |   
  71 | |       const handleTitleChange = React.useCallback((newTitle: string) => {
  72 | |           if (!currentItem) return;
  73 | |           if (isCurrentTask) updateTask(currentItem.id, { title: newTitle });
  74 | |           else updateEvent(currentItem.id, { title: newTitle });
  75 | |       }, [currentItem, isCurrentTask, updateTask, updateEvent]);
  76 | |   
  77 | |       const handleDescChange = React.useCallback((desc: string) => {
  78 | |           if (isReadOnlyCalendar) return;
  79 | |           if (currentTask) updateTask(currentTask.id, { description: desc });
  80 | |           else if (currentEvent) updateEvent(currentEvent.id, { description: desc });
  81 | |       }, [currentTask, currentEvent, isReadOnlyCalendar, updateTask, updateEvent]);
  82 | |   
  83 | |       const handleDelete = React.useCallback((onClose: () => void) => {
  84 | |           if (!currentItem) return;

  87 | |               setEvents((es) => es.filter((e) => e.taskId !== currentItem.id));
  88 | |           } else {
  89 | |               deleteEvent(currentItem.id);
  90 | |           }

  92 | |       }, [currentItem, isCurrentTask, deleteTask, deleteEvent, setEvents]);
  93 | |

  97 | |               return;
  98 | |           }

 104 | |                       setEvents((es) => es.filter((e) => e.taskId !== currentItem.id));
 105 | |                       return ts.filter((x) => x.id !== currentItem.id);

 108 | |               });
 109 | |           } else {

 111 | |                   const e = es.find((x) => x.id === currentItem.id);
 112 | |                   if (e && e.isDraft) return es.filter((x) => x.id !== currentItem.id);
 113 | |                   return es;
 114 | |               });
 115 | |           }
 116 | |   
 117 | |           onClose();
 118 | |       }, [inspectorState, currentItem, isCurrentTask, setTasks, setEvents]);
 119 | |

 44 | |               await loadGoogleIdentityScript();
 45 | |               const code = await requestGoogleAuthCode();

 47 | |

 50 | |                   if (tokens.refreshToken) {
 51 | |                       localStorage.setItem("google_refresh_token", tokens.refreshToken);
 52 | |                   }
 53 | |                   window.location.reload(); // Reload to start sync
 54 | |               }
 55 | |           } catch (error: unknown) {
 56 | |               console.error("Error signing in with Google:", error);
 57 | |               const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
 58 | |               notify(`Sign in failed: ${message}`, "error");
 59 | |           }
 60 | |       };
 61 | |   
 62 | |       const logout = async () => {
 63 | |           try {
 64 | |               localStorage.removeItem("google_access_token");
 65 | |               localStorage.removeItem("google_refresh_token");
 66 | |               setUser(null);
 67 | |               window.location.reload();
 68 | |           } catch (error: unknown) {
 69 | |               console.error("Error signing out:", error);
 70 | |               const message =
 71 | |                   error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
 72 | |               notify(`Logout failed: ${message}`, "error");
 73 | |           }
 74 | |       };
 75 | |   
 76 | |       return (

  26 | ,-> export function TaskListPane({
  27 | |       tasks = [],
  28 | |       setTasks,
  29 | |       filters = [],
  30 | |       setFilters,
  31 | |       sort,
  32 | |       setSort,
  33 | |       query = "",
  34 | |       setQuery,
  35 | |       onDragStart,
  36 | |       activeDragId,
  37 | |       onAddTask,
  38 | |       onDeleteTask,
  39 | |       footer,
  40 | |   }: TaskListPaneProps) {
  41 | |       const updateTask = (id: string, updates: Partial<AppTask>) =>
  42 | |           setTasks((ts) =>
  43 | |               ts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, ...updates } : t)),
  44 | |           );
  45 | |       const deleteTask = (id: string) => {
  46 | |           if (onDeleteTask) {
  47 | |               onDeleteTask(id);
  48 | |           } else {
  49 | |               setTasks((ts) => ts.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  50 | |           }
  51 | |       };
  52 | |   
  53 | |       const allTags = React.useMemo(() => {
  54 | |           const s = new Set<string>();
  55 | |           tasks.forEach((t) => (t.tags || []).forEach((tag) => s.add(tag)));
  56 | |           return Array.from(s).toSorted();
  57 | |       }, [tasks]);
  58 | |   
  59 | |       const filtered = React.useMemo(() => {
  60 | |           let xs = tasks;
  61 | |           for (const f of filters) {
  62 | |               if (!f.column || (!f.value && f.value !== 0)) continue;
  63 | |               xs = xs.filter((t) => {
  64 | |                   let match = false;
  65 | |                   const values = Array.isArray(f.value) ? f.value : [f.value];
  66 | |                   if (values.length === 0) return true;
  67 | |                   
  68 | |                   if (f.column === "status") {
  69 | |                       match = values.includes(t.status || "");
  70 | |                   } else if (f.column === "priority") {
  71 | |                       match = values.includes(t.priority || 0) || values.includes(String(t.priority));
  72 | |                   } else if (f.column === "tag") {
  73 | |                       match = values.some((v) => (t.tags || []).includes(String(v)));
  74 | |                   }
  75 | |                   return f.operator === "is not" ? !match : match;
  76 | |               });
  77 | |           }
  78 | |           if (query.trim()) {
  79 | |               const fuse = new Fuse(xs, {
  80 | |                   keys: ["title", "tags"],
  81 | |                   threshold: 0.4,
  82 | |               });
  83 | |               xs = fuse.search(query).map((result) => result.item);
  84 | |           }
  85 | |           const cmp: Record<string, (a: AppTask, b: AppTask) => number> = {
  86 | |               priority: (a, b) => Number(b.priority || 0) - Number(a.priority || 0),
  87 | |               due: (a, b) => (a.due || "9999").localeCompare(b.due || "9999"),
  88 | |               est: (a, b) => (a.est || 0) - (b.est || 0),
  89 | |               title: (a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title),
  90 | |               added: () => 0,
  91 | |           };
  92 | |           return xs.toSorted(cmp[sort] || (() => 0));
  93 | |       }, [tasks, filters, sort, query]);
  94 | |   
  95 | |       const handleAddTask = () => {
  96 | |           onAddTask(computeFilterDefaults(filters));
  97 | |       };
  98 | |   
  99 | |       return (
 100 | |           <aside className="task-pane">
 101 | |               <header className="task-pane-hd">
 102 | |                   <SearchBar value={query} onChange={setQuery} />
 103 | |                   <div
 104 | |                       className="task-pane-controls"
 105 | |                       style={{
 106 | |                           display: "flex",
 107 | |                           gap: "8px",
 108 | |                           alignItems: "center",
 109 | |                           flexWrap: "wrap",
 110 | |                           width: "100%",
 111 | |                       }}
 112 | |                   >
 113 | |                       <FilterSortButtons
 114 | |                           filters={filters}
 115 | |                           setFilters={setFilters}
 116 | |                           sort={sort}
 117 | |                           setSort={setSort}
 118 | |                           columns={[
 119 | |                               { id: "status", label: "Status" },
 120 | |                               { id: "priority", label: "Priority" },
 121 | |                               { id: "tag", label: "Tag" },
 122 | |                           ]}
 123 | |                           getValuesForColumn={(col: string) => {
 124 | |                               if (col === "status")
 125 | |                                   return ["todo", "next-up", "doing", "done"];
 126 | |                               if (col === "priority") return ["0", "1", "2", "3", "4"];
 127 | |                               if (col === "tag") return allTags;
 128 | |                               return [];
 129 | |                           }}
 130 | |                           sortOptions={[
 131 | |                               { id: "priority", label: "Priority" },
 132 | |                               { id: "due", label: "Due Date" },
 133 | |                               { id: "est", label: "Estimate" },
 134 | |                               { id: "title", label: "Title" },
 135 | |                               { id: "added", label: "Date Added" },
 136 | |                           ]}
 137 | |                       />

 149 | |                               alignItems: "center",
 150 | |                               justifyContent: "center",
 151 | |                           }}
 152 | |                           title="Add Task"

 157 | |                   </div>
 158 | |               </header>
 159 | |

 15 | ,-> export function TipsList() {

 40 | |                               setTips((ts) => ts.filter((_, j) => j !== i))

 43 | |                       >
 44 | |                           ×
 45 | |                       </button>
 46 | |                   </li>

 56 | |                           onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}

 104 | ,-> function TaskCanvasInner({ tasks, setTasks }: TaskCanvasProps) {
 105 | |       const { screenToFlowPosition } = useReactFlow();
 106 | |   
 107 | |       const canvasTasks = useMemo(() => tasks.filter((t) => t.onCanvas), [tasks]);
 108 | |   
 109 | |       const nodes: Node[] = useMemo(() => {
 110 | |           return canvasTasks.map((t) => ({
 111 | |               id: t.id,
 112 | |               type: "taskNode",
 113 | |               position: { x: t.canvasX || 0, y: t.canvasY || 0 },
 114 | |               data: { task: t },
 115 | |           }));
 116 | |       }, [canvasTasks]);
 117 | |   
 118 | |       const edges: Edge[] = useMemo(() => {
 119 | |           const newEdges: Edge[] = [];
 120 | |   
 121 | |           // Create edges from tasks data
 122 | |           for (const t of canvasTasks) {
 123 | |               if (
 124 | |                   t.parent_task &&
 125 | |                   canvasTasks.some((ct) => ct.id === t.parent_task)
 126 | |               ) {
 127 | |                   newEdges.push({
 128 | |                       id: `e-${t.parent_task}-child-${t.id}`,
 129 | |                       source: t.parent_task,
 130 | |                       sourceHandle: "child",
 131 | |                       target: t.id,
 132 | |                       targetHandle: "parent",
 133 | |                       animated: true,
 134 | |                       style: { stroke: "var(--accent)" },
 135 | |                   });
 136 | |               }
 137 | |   
 138 | |               if (t.dependencies) {
 139 | |                   for (const depId of t.dependencies) {
 140 | |                       if (canvasTasks.some((ct) => ct.id === depId)) {
 141 | |                           newEdges.push({
 142 | |                               id: `e-${depId}-dependent-${t.id}`,
 143 | |                               source: depId,
 144 | |                               sourceHandle: "dependent",
 145 | |                               target: t.id,
 146 | |                               targetHandle: "dependency",
 147 | |                               style: { stroke: "#d9866b" },
 148 | |                           });
 149 | |                       }
 150 | |                   }
 151 | |               }
 152 | |           }
 153 | |   
 154 | |           return newEdges;
 155 | |       }, [canvasTasks]);
 156 | |   
 157 | |       const onNodesChange = useCallback(
 158 | |           (changes: NodeChange[]) => {
 159 | |               // We only care about position changes to persist them
 160 | |               setTasks((prev) => {
 161 | |                   let updated = [...prev];
 162 | |                   for (const change of changes) {
 163 | |                       if (change.type === "position" && change.position) {
 164 | |                           const pos = change.position;
 165 | |                           updated = updated.map((t) =>
 166 | |                               t.id === change.id
 167 | |                                   ? {
 168 | |                                         ...t,
 169 | |                                         canvasX: pos.x,
 170 | |                                         canvasY: pos.y,
 171 | |                                     }
 172 | |                                   : t,
 173 | |                           );
 174 | |                       }
 175 | |                   }
 176 | |                   return updated;
 177 | |               });
 178 | |           },
 179 | |           [setTasks],
 180 | |       );
 181 | |   
 182 | |       const isValidConnection = useCallback((connection: Connection | Edge) => {
 183 | |           if (
 184 | |               connection.sourceHandle === "child" &&
 185 | |               connection.targetHandle === "parent"
 186 | |           )
 187 | |               return true;
 188 | |           if (
 189 | |               connection.sourceHandle === "dependent" &&
 190 | |               connection.targetHandle === "dependency"
 191 | |           )
 192 | |               return true;
 193 | |           return false;
 194 | |       }, []);
 195 | |   
 196 | |       const onConnect = useCallback(
 197 | |           (connection: Connection) => {
 198 | |               if (!isValidConnection(connection)) return;
 199 | |   
 200 | |               setTasks((prev) =>
 201 | |                   prev.map((t) => {
 202 | |                       // connecting source (parent) bottom to target (child) top
 203 | |                       if (
 204 | |                           connection.target === t.id &&
 205 | |                           connection.targetHandle === "parent" &&
 206 | |                           connection.sourceHandle === "child"
 207 | |                       ) {
 208 | |                           return { ...t, parent_task: connection.source };
 209 | |                       }
 210 | |                       // connecting source (dependency) right to target (dependent) left
 211 | |                       if (
 212 | |                           connection.target === t.id &&
 213 | |                           connection.targetHandle === "dependency" &&
 214 | |                           connection.sourceHandle === "dependent"
 215 | |                       ) {
 216 | |                           const deps = t.dependencies || [];
 217 | |                           if (!deps.includes(connection.source)) {
 218 | |                               return {
 219 | |                                   ...t,
 220 | |                                   dependencies: [...deps, connection.source],
 221 | |                               };
 222 | |                           }
 223 | |                       }
 224 | |                       return t;
 225 | |                   }),
 226 | |               );
 227 | |           },
 228 | |           [setTasks, isValidConnection],
 229 | |       );
 230 | |   
 231 | |       const onDoubleClick = useCallback(
 232 | |           (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
 233 | |               if (!(e.target instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
 234 | |               if (e.target.closest(".react-flow__node")) return; // Do not create task if clicking on an existing node
 235 | |   
 236 | |               // Project screen coordinates to flow coordinates
 237 | |               const position = screenToFlowPosition({
 238 | |                   x: e.clientX,
 239 | |                   y: e.clientY,
 240 | |               });
 241 | |   
 242 | |               const id = `t${Date.now()}`;
 243 | |               const newTask: Task = {
 244 | |                   id,
 245 | |                   title: "New Task",
 246 | |                   status: "todo",
 247 | |                   priority: 1,
 248 | |                   tags: [],
 249 | |                   subtasks: [],

 255 | |                   canvasX: position.x,
 256 | |                   canvasY: position.y,
 257 | |               };
 258 | |   
 259 | |               setTasks((ts) => [...ts, newTask]);
 260 | |           },
 261 | |           [setTasks, screenToFlowPosition],

 269 | |                   left: 0,

  15 | ,-> export function Kanban() {
  16 | |       const [tasks, setTasks] = useTasks();
  17 | |       const [drag, setDrag] = React.useState<{ taskId: string; x: number; y: number; overCol: string | null } | null>(null);
  18 | |   
  19 | |       const onTaskDown = (task: AppTask) => (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
  20 | |           if (e.button !== 0) return;
  21 | |           e.preventDefault();
  22 | |           const start = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
  23 | |           let active = false;
  24 | |           const move = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
  25 | |               if (
  26 | |                   !active &&
  27 | |                   Math.hypot(ev.clientX - start.x, ev.clientY - start.y) < 5
  28 | |               )
  29 | |                   return;
  30 | |               active = true;
  31 | |               const el = document.elementFromPoint(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
  32 | |               const colEl = el instanceof Element ? el.closest("[data-kanban-col]") : null;
  33 | |               setDrag({
  34 | |                   taskId: task.id,
  35 | |                   x: ev.clientX,
  36 | |                   y: ev.clientY,
  37 | |                   overCol: colEl instanceof HTMLElement ? (colEl.dataset?.kanbanCol || null) : null,
  38 | |               });
  39 | |           };
  40 | |           const up = (ev: PointerEvent) => {
  41 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
  42 | |               window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
  43 | |               if (active) {
  44 | |                   const el = document.elementFromPoint(ev.clientX, ev.clientY);
  45 | |                   const colEl = el instanceof Element ? el.closest("[data-kanban-col]") : null;
  46 | |                   if (
  47 | |                       colEl instanceof HTMLElement &&
  48 | |                       colEl.dataset.kanbanCol !== task.status
  49 | |                   ) {
  50 | |                       setTasks((ts: AppTask[]) =>
  51 | |                           ts.map((t) =>
  52 | |                               t.id === task.id
  53 | |                                   ? {
  54 | |                                         ...t,
  55 | |                                         status: colEl.dataset.kanbanCol,
  56 | |                                     }
  57 | |                                   : t,
  58 | |                           ),
  59 | |                       );
  60 | |                   }
  61 | |               }
  62 | |               setDrag(null);
  63 | |           };
  64 | |           window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
  65 | |           window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
  66 | |       };
  67 | |   
  68 | |       return (
  69 | |           <div className="kanban">
  70 | |               <div className="kanban-cols">
  71 | |                   {KANBAN_COLUMNS.map((col) => {
  72 | |                       const colTasks = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === col.id);
  73 | |                       const isOver = drag?.overCol === col.id;
  74 | |                       return (
  75 | |                           <div
  76 | |                               key={col.id}
  77 | |                               data-kanban-col={col.id}
  78 | |                               className={`kanban-col ${col.accent ? "is-accent" : ""} ${col.dim ? "is-dim" : ""} ${isOver ? "is-drag-over" : ""}`}
  79 | |                           >
  80 | |                               <header className="kanban-col-hd">
  81 | |                                   <span className="bracket">
  82 | |                                       {col.accent ? "▸" : "◇"}
  83 | |                                   </span>
  84 | |                                   <span className="kanban-col-name">
  85 | |                                       {col.label}
  86 | |                                   </span>
  87 | |                                   <span className="kanban-col-count">
  88 | |                                       {colTasks.length}
  89 | |                                   </span>
  90 | |                               </header>
  91 | |                               <div className="kanban-col-body">
  92 | |                                   {colTasks.length === 0 ? (
  93 | |                                       <div className="kanban-col-empty">
  94 | |                                           <span className="dim">
  95 | |                                               {isOver ? "▸ drop here" : "—"}
  96 | |                                           </span>
  97 | |                                       </div>
  98 | |                                   ) : (
  99 | |                                       colTasks.map((task) => (
 100 | |                                           <KanbanCard
 101 | |                                               key={task.id}
 102 | |                                               task={task}
 103 | |                                               onPointerDown={onTaskDown(task)}
 104 | |                                               dragging={drag?.taskId === task.id}
 105 | |                                           />
 106 | |                                       ))
 107 | |                                   )}
 108 | |                               </div>
 109 | |                           </div>
 110 | |                       );
 111 | |                   })}
 112 | |               </div>
 113 | |   
 114 | |               {drag && (
 115 | |                   <div
 116 | |                       className="kanban-ghost"
 117 | |                       style={{ left: drag.x + 12, top: drag.y - 6 }}
 118 | |                   >
 119 | |                       <div
 120 | |                           className={`task-circle pri-bg-${tasks.find((t) => t.id === drag.taskId)?.priority}`}

 122 | |                       <span className="kanban-ghost-title">
 123 | |                           {tasks.find((t) => t.id === drag.taskId)?.title}
 124 | |                       </span>
 125 | |                   </div>
 126 | |               )}
 127 | |           </div>
 128 | |       );
